Reference root: /home/csl/Desktop/odoo_18/extra_addons/ibsolar/<module>. New module: /home/csl/Desktop/odoo_18/demo_modules/odoo_demo_env/advance_manufacturing (files shown as `advance_manufacturing/...`).

# Gap analysis E: reference modules vs advance_manufacturing

Reference root: extra_addons/ibsolar. Target: demo_modules/odoo_demo_env/advance_manufacturing (read in full with IMPLEMENTATION_LOG.md). That module covers unit-by-unit manufacturing only (serials, stages, QC, grades, pallets, company+group switch). It has no sale/purchase/invoice customisation, no emails or WhatsApp, no res.users recipient flags, no maintenance. So everything below is TO-BUILD unless marked otherwise.

| machine_product_maintenance | whatsapp | Templates and composer for 4 notifications | Yes: thin own outbound-message service or email only |

| ib_purchase, ib_purchase_approval, ib_invoice | whatsapp (runtime, hard-coded ids 28, 33, 34, 36, 0) | Approval, confirm, cancel, invoice notices | Yes, same as above |
| ib_purchase_approval | Studio view customisation | Priority comment | Yes: drop |
